```
def project_feasible(h,n):
 h=np.clip(h,0,1)
 target=n/2.0
 for _ in range(max(50,n)):
   diff=target-h.sum()
   if abs(diff)<1e-12: break
   if diff>0:
     mask=h<1-1e-15
     if not mask.any(): break
     add=diff/np.count_nonzero(mask)
     # if add> room for some, cap? Proportional room may better.
     h[mask]=np.minimum(1.0,h[mask]+add)
   else:
     mask=h>1e-15
     if not mask.any(): break
     h[mask]/= (h[mask].sum()/diff?) Actually subtract prop: h[mask]=np.maximum(0,h[mask]+diff/h[mask].sum()*h[mask])? 
```

Implement `cd_fast` with parameter `gold=16` and `recompute_every=3`.

Pseudo:
```
def cd_fast(h,n,passes,deadline,gold=16,recomp=3):
  h=h.copy(); c=corr(h,n); best=max(c)*2/n
  improved_global=False
  for pass in range(passes):
    if time.time()>deadline: break
    improved=False
    for p in range(n):
      if time.time()>deadline: break
      h0=h[p]
      if h0<=1e-14 or h0>=1-1e-14: continue
      S=max(p,n-1-p)
      m_lo=n-1-S; m_hi=n-1+S
      # within full always? S<=n-1 so m_lo>=0,m_hi<=2n-2
      ms=np.arange(m_lo,m_hi+1)
      s=ms-(n-1)
      aff=c[ms].copy()
      a=np.zeros(len(ms))
      vh=p-s
      mask=(vh>=0)&(vh<n)
      if np.any(mask): a[mask] += 1.0 - h[vh[mask]]
      vl=p+s
      mask=(vl>=0)&(vl<n)
      if np.any(mask): a[mask] -= h[vl[mask]]
      di=S  # since ms= n-1-S.., s=0 at index S
      a[di]=0.0
      diag_base=aff[di]
      U=0.0
      if m_lo>0: U=max(U, float(c[:m_lo].max()))
      if m_hi+1<2*n-1: U=max(U, float(c[m_hi+1:].max()))
      lo=-h0; hi=1.0-h0
      f0=U? but max of aff max = max(c) >= U. Actually f0=float(np.max(aff)) maybe not if U larger? f0=max(U,np.max(aff)).
      # golden
      best_d=0.0; best_f=f0
      gr=0.6180339887498949
      d1=hi-gr*(hi-lo); d2=lo+gr*(hi-lo)
      f1=f(d1); f2=f(d2)
      if f1<best_f: best...
      if f2<best_f: ...
      for _ in range(gold):
        if hi-lo < 1e-15: break
        if f1 < f2:
          hi=d2; d2=d1; f2=f1; d1=hi-gr*(hi-lo); f1=f(d1)
        else:
          lo=d1; d1=d2; f1=f2; d2=lo+gr*(hi-lo); f2=f(d2)
        if f1<best_f: best_d=d1; best_f=f1
        if f2<best_f: best_d=d2; best_f=f2
      # endpoints already? d=lo,hi? initial best d=0; maybe evaluate bounds
      for cand in (lo,hi,0.0): compute f
      if best_f < f0 - 1e-12:
        # But ensure feasible? best_d within
        h[p]=h0+best_d
        c[ms] += a*best_d
        c[n-1] = diag_base + best_d*(1.0 - 2*h0 - best_d)
        if c[n-1] < 0 and c[n-1] > -1e-10: c[n-1]=0
        improved=True
    # update best c? 
    cur=float(np.max(c))*2/n
    if cur<best: best=cur
    if pass>0 and pass%recomp==0:
      c=corr(h,n)
    # if not improved break
    if not improved: break
  # final recompute
  h=proj(h,n); c=corr(h,n); return h,max(c)*2/n
```

Binary local search maybe more robust than L-BFGS. Let's design simpler binary "MaxCount greedy pair" using c and active lags.
Given binary a and c = correlate(a,1-a). Counts integer. Objective M=max(c). Need reduce M. Since dx same. We can attempt local search to reduce M by finding swaps that ensure all active lags counts decrease and inactive not exceed M-1 (if aiming lower) or just improve max.
Algorithm:
- Maintain a, c. M=max(c).
- While time:
  - Active indices = np.where(c >= M - max(1, (M//100)))? Counts integer. For objective to reduce to M-1, need all c at M → M-1 or less, and no c increase to M.
  - Find candidate bits that contribute to active lags. For each active m, which p contribute? We can compute a list of bad positions: For each p with a[p]=1, count number of active lags where p is in a 1->0 pair. If high, flipping/moving it may reduce several.
  - For candidate source p (1) and destination q (0), apply swap and evaluate new M (max c). If improved keep; else reject.
Search space large; random sampling of source/dest based on scores.
We can compute source score quickly? For active m, contribution of p to c[m] if `p-s` high role? For full correlate formula c_m=∑ h_{n+k?}; count 1->0. A position p with a[p]=1 contributes if high role: n=p? Wait c_m counts h_{n+s?} (1-h_n) where high=1 low=0. For p as high with 1: need low index p-s has 0. For p as low with 1: c term has (1-h_p)=0, so 1 at low does not contribute. But in full c, p as high when p-s valid and a[p-s]=0. So for active m (s), bad source p are indices p such that a[p]=1 and either a[p-s]=0 (if high) for that m. That's the "1 followed by 0 at distance -s"? Direct.
We can compute score for each p by summing over active m/s: `score[p] += (a[p] & (1-a[p-s])) + (a[p] & (1-a[p+s]?)?` Need count how active c entries involve p as the 1. Derivative A for 1->0 (dh=-1): Δ = -A. c decreases if A>0. A = high_role (1-a[p-s]) + low_role(-a[p+s]). For a[p]=1, high part positive when left low zero; low part negative when right one. For 1->0, if right one, c_s increases (because term h_right*(1-h_p) becomes 1). So source score = sum positive A over active, minus potential negatives. Hard.
Maybe random scanning enough: choose active m, pick a random p that contributes to it (i.e., a[p]=1 and appropriate neighbor 0/1?). Then choose random destination 0. But could be many.

```
def binary_swap_search(a,c,n,deadline):
  best_a=a.copy(); best_c=max(c)
  ones=np.flatnonzero(a==1); zeros=np.flatnonzero(a==0)
  rng ...
  for it in range(...):
    if time> deadline: break
    M=best_c? current
    # choose source among ones, dest zeros randomly; maybe weighted by a heuristic: if len active small, use contributions.
    i=rng.choice(ones); j=rng.choice(zeros)
    # apply swap i 1->0, j 0->1
    apply_flip(i,-1); apply_flip(j,+1)
    newM=int(c.max())
    if newM < best_c:
       best_c=newM; best_a=a.copy()
    else:
       # revert in reverse order
       apply_flip(j,-1); apply_flip(i,+1)
    # occasionally if no improvement for many, accept equal? Maybe no.
  return best_a, best_c*dx
```

Could generate Thue-Morse via iterative concat: start [0], repeat: `t = np.concatenate([t, 1-t])` until length >=n. This is fine. But no long literal. Use loop double.
```
def thue_bits(n):
  if n<=0: return np.array([])
  t=np.array([0],dtype=int)
  while len(t)<n: t=np.concatenate((t,1-t))
  return t[:n]
```
For large n 800, cheap. Could use as binary start.

Let's detail functions:

```
import numpy as np, time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def proj(h,n):
    h=np.asarray(h,dtype=float).copy()
    h=np.clip(h,0,1)
    target=n/2.0
    for _ in range(max(50,int(n*2))):
        diff=target-h.sum()
        if abs(diff)<1e-13: break
        if diff>0:
            mask=h<1.0-1e-15
            if not mask.any(): break
            room=(1.0-h[mask])
            tot=room.sum()
            if tot<=1e-15: break
            add=diff/tot*room
            h[mask]+=add
        else:
            mask=h>1e-15
            if not mask.any(): break
            tot=h[mask].sum()
            if tot<=1e-15: break
            h[mask]=h[mask]*(1.0+diff/tot) # subtract proportional; could clip below 0
            np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
    # final exact correction on fractional entries
    diff=target-h.sum()
    if abs(diff)>1e-14:
       mask=(h>1e-9)&(h<1-1e-9)
       if mask.any():
          h[mask]+=diff/mask.sum()
          np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
       else:
          h+=diff/n
          np.clip(h,0,1,out=h)
    return h
```
Proportional subtract: `h[mask] += diff/tot * h[mask]` (diff negative), then clip. Good.
Potential infinite if diff small but saturation? okay.

`corr(h,n): return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode='full')`
`objective(h,n): return float(np.max(corr(h,n)) * (2.0/n))`

```
def cd_eval(aff, coeff, diag_base, diag_h0, di, unaff, d):
    vals = aff + coeff*d
    vals[di] = diag_base + d*(1.0 - 2.0*diag_h0 - d)
    r = vals.max()
    if unaff > r: r = unaff
    return float(r)
```

Now smooth L-BFGS:
```
def smooth_obj(x, n, alpha, rho):
    dx=2.0/n
    c=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')
    m=c.max()
    # logsumexp stable
    if alpha <= 1:
       lse=m
    else:
       s=(c-m)/alpha
       lse=m + alpha*(s.max()+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s-s.max())))) # s.max=0
    return dx*lse + rho*(x.sum()-n/2.0)**2
```
Actually if s=(c-m)/alpha, max s=0, lse = m + alpha*log(sum(exp(s))). Good.
Could c max negative? no.
Bounds. `minimize` top-level. In `smooth_lbfgs`, use `best_h`, alphas maybe [4000,800,150]. For each alpha, rho maybe 1e4. But if x sum drift, obj penalty large; L-BFGS may first fix sum. Good.

To avoid inner function, implement `apply_flip(c,a,n,p,dh)` modifies arrays. It recomputes S, ms, s, coeff (with a old), `np.multiply(coeff,dh,out=tmp)`, `c[ms]+=tmp`. Diagonal no change (dh=±1 binary gives 0) except if a not strictly binary? For binary search yes. But if a[p] changes, after update. Helper:
```
def apply_flip(a,c,n,p,dh):
  val = int(a[p])
  if val == 1 and dh >=0: return False
  if val == 0 and dh <=0: return False
  S=max(p,n-1-p); m0=n-1-S; ms=np.arange(m0,n-1+S+1); s=ms-(n-1)
  coeff=np.zeros(len(ms))
  vh=p-s; mask=...; coeff[mask]+=1-a[vh[mask]]
  vl=p+s; mask=...; coeff[mask]-=a[vl[mask]]
  coeff[S]=0
  tmp=coeff*dh
  c[ms]+=tmp
  # diag no change? For binary, old h(1-h)=0, new 0. If a strictly 0/1, yes.
  a[p]=1-val
  return True
```
But if a contains floats? Use binary `a=(h>0.5).astype(float)`, sum adjusted. Good.
For binary, c should be updated for all ms including diagonal? coeff[S]=0; diagonal old 0, new 0. Good.
`c` may drift; recompute periodically.
Swap candidate: choose i,j. Apply i dh=-1, j dh=+1. If improve, keep; else apply j -1, i +1 to revert. However after applying j, a[i]=0,a[j]=1; to revert: flip j -1 (1->0) then i +1 (0->1). Good.
Need choose i,j. Simple random with scoring? We can compute active M as current max. If current M = best? We'll use random weighted:
```

 return best_a, best_c*2/n
```

Binary local search function:
```
def binary_targeted(a,n,deadline,max_iters=20000):
 a=a.copy(); c=corr(a,n); best_a=a.copy(); best_c=int(c.max()); rng=np.random.RandomState(1); stagn=0
 for it in range(max_iters):
   if time.time()>deadline: break
   if stagn>500: # recompute active maybe
     c=corr(a,n); best_c=min(best_c,int(c.max()))
   active=np.where(c>=best_c)[0]
   if len(active)==0: break
   m=int(active[int(rng.randint(len(active)))])
   s=m-(n-1)
   lo=max(0,s); hi=min(n,n+s)
   if hi<=lo: continue
   ps=np.arange(lo,hi)
   ps=ps[(a[ps]>0.5)&(a[ps-s]<=0.5)]
   if len(ps)==0:
      # maybe active diag or all contributions none due to c not exactly? Break/recompute
      stagn+=10
      if stagn>200: c=corr(a,n); best_c=int(c.max()); stagn=0
      continue
   p=int(ps[int(rng.randint(len(ps)))]); q=p-s
   if q<0 or q>=n or a[q]>0.5: continue
   apply_flip(a,c,n,p,-1); apply_flip(a,c,n,q,1)
   new=int(c.max())
   if new<best_c:
      best_c=new; best_a=a.copy(); stagn=0
   else:
      apply_flip(a,c,n,q,-1); apply_flip(a,c,n,p,1)
      stagn+=1
   if it%200==0: c=corr(a,n)
 return best_a, best_c*2/n
```

Start generation for binary from continuous: `a = (h >=0.5).astype(float); cnt=a.sum(); target=n//2; if cnt>target: flip largest?` Use h values to adjust:
```
def to_binary(h,n):
 a=(h>=0.5).astype(float); target=n//2
 if a.sum()<target:
   idx=np.argsort(h[a==0]); a[idx[:target-a.sum()]]? Wait idx already among zeros? simpler:

 else: order=np.argsort(h); ...
```
Better round with exact count: choose largest n/2 h as 1. This preserves information.
```
def round_balanced(h,n):
  target=n//2
  order=np.argsort(h)
  a=np.zeros(n); a[order[-target:]]=1.0
  return a
```
If n odd, target floor? Sum constraint should n/2 not integer; binary cannot exact. For binary search need even n (target integer). We can only run binary for even n. If odd, skip or use target floor? The sum constraint must be n/2; binary impossible for odd. We can use n even only. Good.

But the continuous h can have sum n/2 odd half; if n odd, binary not exact. Return continuous.

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):

 # remove odd? maybe if odd, no binary but continuous okay. remove >1200 maybe.
 best_h=None; best_c=inf
 # define make_start

    rem=(deadline-time.time())
    if rem<50: break
    # allocate share = rem/(len(n_list)-idx) but cap maybe 300 for large
    share = min(300.0, rem / max(1, len(n_list)-idx))
    dl = time.time()+share
    if n==n_par and h_par is not None:
      h=proj(h_par,n)
    else:
      # interpolate parent if available
      if h_par is not None and n_par>0:
        x0=np.linspace(0,2,n_par); x1=np.linspace(0,2,n)
        h=np.interp(x1,x0,h_par)
      else:
        h=0.5+0.4*rng.randn(n)
      h=proj(h,n)
    # maybe add small noise to parent to explore
    h_noisy=proj(h + 0.01*rng.randn(n), n) if n<=900 else h
    # run CD on h
    h1,c1=cd_fast(h,n,passes_est(n,share//2),dl, gold=14)
    if c1<best_c: best_h, best_c=h1,c1
    # try noisy CD with remaining if time
    if time.time()<dl-20:

    # try binary if even and time

       a2,c2b = binary_targeted(a,n,time.time()+min(120, (deadline-time.time())*0.2))

    # smooth L-BFGS if time and n<=700

    # not easy time per pass. Set passes 50 but deadline.
    if c4<best: best=h4,c4
 best_h=proj(best_h,len(best_h)); best_c=objective(best_h,len)
 return best_h,best_c,len(best_h)
```

```
def cd_diagnosis(t, z_start, b_pos, a_pos, b_neg, a_neg, lo, hi, diag_base, diag_h0):
    if t < z_start - 1e-12: return False, lo, hi
    low=lo; high=hi
    if b_pos.size:
       high = min(high, float(np.min((t - b_pos)/a_pos)))
    if b_neg.size:
       low = max(low, float(np.max((t - b_neg)/a_neg)))
    if low > high + 1e-14: return False, lo, hi
    # diag
    qmax = diag_base + 0.25 - diag_h0*diag_h0
    if qmax <= t + 1e-12:
       return True, low, high
    disc = (1.0-2.0*diag_h0)**2 + 4.0*(diag_base - t)
    if disc < 0: # due round, all? then infeasible? If qmax>t, disc >0.
       sqrt=0.0
    else: sqrt=np.sqrt(disc)
    qb=1.0-2.0*diag_h0
    r1=(qb - sqrt)/2.0; r2=(qb + sqrt)/2.0
    # Need [low,high] not entirely inside [r1,r2]
    if high < r1 or low > r2:
       return True, low, high
    # It intersects forbidden; need point outside. Feasible if low < r1 or high > r2 (with eps)
    if low < r1 - 1e-14 or high > r2 + 1e-14:
       # interval feasible part maybe [low,min(high,r1)] or [max(low,r2),high]
       # return chosen interval? For feasibility true.
       return True, low, high
    return False, lo, hi
```

```
def cd_check(a_pos,b_pos,a_neg,b_neg,z_start,diag_base,diag_h0,lo,hi,t):
    if t < z_start - 1e-12:
        return False, lo, hi, 0.0, 0.0
    low=lo; high=hi
    if b_pos.size:
        ub=float(np.min((t-b_pos)/a_pos))
        if ub < high: high=ub
    if b_neg.size:
        lb=float(np.max((t-b_neg)/a_neg))
        if lb > low: low=lb
    if low > high + 1e-12:
        return False, lo, hi, 0.0, 0.0
    qb=1.0-2.0*diag_h0
    qmax=diag_base + 0.25 - diag_h0*diag_h0
    if qmax <= t + 1e-12:
        return True, low, high, 0.0, 0.0
    disc=qb*qb + 4.0*(diag_base - t)
    if disc < 0.0: disc=0.0
    rt=np.sqrt(disc)
    r1=(qb-rt)*0.5; r2=(qb+rt)*0.5
    if high < r1 or low > r2:
        return True, low, high, r1, r2
    if low < r1 - 1e-13 or high > r2 + 1e-13:
        return True, low, high, r1, r2
    return False, lo, hi, r1, r2
```

Let's write code mentally.

```
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize

def project(h,n): ...
def corr(h,n): return np.correlate(h,1.0-h,mode='full')
def c5_of(h,n): return float(np.max(corr(h,n))*(2.0/n))

def cd_eval(aff,coeff,diag_base,diag_h0,di,unaff,d):
    vals=aff+coeff*d
    vals[di]=diag_base + d*(1.0-2.0*diag_h0-d)
    r=float(vals.max())
    if unaff>r: r=unaff
    return r

def cd_check(...): ...

def cd_fast(h,n,max_passes,deadline,recomp=3):
    h=np.array(h,dtype=float,copy=True)
    h=project(h,n)
    c=corr(h,n)
    best_c=float(np.max(c))*(2.0/n)
    passed=0
    for _ in range(max_passes):
       if time.time()>deadline: break
       improved=False
       for p in range(n):
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          h0=float(h[p])
          if h0 <= 1e-13 or h0 >= 1.0-1e-13: continue
          S = p if p >= n-1-p else n-1-p
          m0 = n-1-S
          m1 = n-1+S
          ms = np.arange(m0,m1+1)
          s = ms - (n-1)
          aff = c[ms].copy()
          coeff = np.zeros(len(ms))
          vh = p - s
          mask = (vh >= 0) & (vh < n)
          if np.any(mask): coeff[mask] += 1.0 - h[vh[mask]]
          vl = p + s
          mask = (vl >= 0) & (vl < n)
          if np.any(mask): coeff[mask] -= h[vl[mask]]
          di = S
          coeff[di] = 0.0
          diag_base = float(c[n-1])
          unaff = 0.0
          if m0 > 0: unaff=max(unaff,float(c[:m0].max()))
          if m1 + 1 < 2*n-1: unaff=max(unaff,float(c[m1+1:].max()))
          f0 = max(unaff, float(aff.max()))
          lo = -h0; hi = 1.0 - h0
          posm = coeff > 1e-14
          negm = coeff < -1e-14
          a_pos = coeff[posm]; b_pos = aff[posm]
          a_neg = coeff[negm]; b_neg = aff[negm]
          zerom = ~(posm | negm); zerom[di]=False
          b_zero_max = float(aff[zerom].max()) if np.any(zerom) else -1e100
          z_start = max(unaff, b_zero_max, 0.0)
          t_lo = z_start; t_hi = f0
          feas,low,high,r1,r2 = False,lo,hi,0.0,0.0
          if t_hi <= t_lo + 1e-14:
             feas,low,high,r1,r2 = cd_check(...,t_hi)
          else:
             for __ in range(22):
                tmid = 0.5*(t_lo+t_hi)
                feasible, _,_,_,_ = cd_check(..., tmid)
                if feasible: t_hi=tmid
                else: t_lo=tmid
             feas,low,high,r1,r2 = cd_check(..., t_hi)
             if not feas:
                feas,low,high,r1,r2 = cd_check(..., f0)
          cands = (lo, hi, 0.0, 0.5*(lo+hi), 0.5 - h0)
          cands += (low, high, 0.5*(low+high), max(lo, min(high, r1)), min(hi, max(low, r2)), min(hi, r1-1e-9), max(lo, r2+1e-9))
          best_d=0.0; best_f=f0
          for d in cands:
             if d < lo: d=lo
             elif d > hi: d=hi
             f = cd_eval(aff, coeff, diag_base, h0, di, unaff, d)
             if f < best_f:
                best_f=f; best_d=d
          if best_f < f0 - 1e-12:
             h[p] = h0 + best_d
             c[ms] = aff + coeff*best_d
             c[n-1] = diag_base + best_d*(1.0 - 2.0*h0 - best_d)
             if c[n-1] < 0.0 and c[n-1] > -1e-10: c[n-1]=0.0
             improved = True
       passed += 1
       if passed % recomp == 0:
          c = corr(h,n)
       cur = float(np.max(c))
       if cur > 0: curs = cur* (2/n) else 0
       if cur*(2/n) < best_c: best_c=...
       if not improved: break
    h=project(h,n)
    c=corr(h,n)
    return h, float(np.max(c))*2.0/n
```
Potential bug: `c[ms] = aff + coeff*best_d` resets affected entries to base+linear; but what about entries outside? fine. For non-diag exact. For diag, we set after. But if `ms` includes diag, we set `aff+coeff*d` with coeff[di]=0 so c diag base, then set exact. Good.
`c[ms] = ...` length matches if m0,m1 within [0,2n-2]; S max n-1, m0>=0,m1<=2n-2.
`unaff` if no outside set 0. But counts nonnegative; if outside exist max maybe 0. Good.
`f0` from aff max/unaff. If `c` has tiny negative, f0 maybe 0? Use max 0.
Binary feasibility: `t_lo` maybe > f0 due to numerical? z_start could >f0? z_start is max constant unaffected and b_zero (non-diag constants) and unaff; should ≤f0 because f0 max of all affected and unaff, includes those constants. If diagonals excluded, diag base could >f0? No f0 includes aff max including diag base. z_start excludes diag. Good.
`t_hi` maybe lower than qmax? feasible handles.

The candidate `min(hi, r1-1e-9)` if r1=0 and no diag restriction? Could be negative; clipped. `max(lo, r2+1e-9)`. OK.
Need if `r1,r2` are zeros when no diag; duplicates. fine.

`apply_flip` signature: `def apply_flip(a,c,n,p,dh):`
```
val = 1 if a[p] > 0.5 else 0
if val == 1 and dh >=0: return
if val == 0 and dh <0: return
S = max(p,n-1-p)
ms=np.arange(n-1-S, n-1+S+1)
s=ms-(n-1)
coeff=np.zeros(len(ms));
vh=p-s; mask...; coeff[mask] += 1-a[vh[mask]]; ...
coeff[S]=0
c[ms] += coeff*dh
# diagonal no change; but if a was not exact binary, maybe set? We'll restrict.
a[p] = 1.0 - val
```
For c0: if val 0, new 1 diag contribution 0; old 0. Good.
But if other h fractional? not used.

`binary_targeted`:
```
def binary_targeted(a,n,deadline,max_iters=20000,seed=7):
 a=np.array(a,dtype=float,copy=True)
 a[(a>0.5)&(a<1)]? already 0/1.
 target=n//2
 if int(a.sum()) != target: a=round_balanced(a*? or ones? Use h? We'll pass h to round first.
 c=corr(a,n); curM=int(np.max(c)); best_c=curM; best_a=a.copy()
 rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
 stagn=0
 for it in range(max_iters):
   if time.time()>deadline: break
   if it % 100 == 99:
      c=corr(a,n)
   curM=int(np.max(c))
   if curM < best_c: best_c=curM; best_a=a.copy()
   # if no moves? 
   if curM <=0: break
   active = np.where(c >= curM)[0]
   if active.size==0: break
   m=int(active[rng.randint(active.size)])
   s=m-(n-1)
   lo=max(0,s); hi=min(n,n+s)
   if hi<=lo: continue
   ps=np.arange(lo,hi)
   # Need low index p-s valid; by lo/hi, but still for negative s p-s up to n-1. ok.
   ps=ps[(a[ps] > 0.5) & (a[ps-s] <= 0.5)]
   if ps.size==0:
      stagn += 5
      # If active entries have no contributions? maybe due to max at c0 (should 0) or numeric; recompute
      if stagn > 100:
         c=corr(a,n); curM=int(np.max(c)); best_c=min(best_c,curM); break
      continue
   p=int(ps[rng.randint(ps.size)]); q=p-s
   if q<0 or q>=n or a[q]>0.5 or p==q:
      continue
   apply_flip(a,c,n,p,-1.0)
   apply_flip(a,c,n,q,1.0)
   newM=int(np.max(c))
   if newM < curM:
      if newM < best_c: best_c=newM; best_a=a.copy()
      stagn=0
   elif newM == curM and (stagn > 200 and rng.rand() < 0.1):
      # accept equal as random walk, but ensure not worse
      # set curM same
      if rng.rand() < 0.02: # sometimes snapshot? no
         pass
      # keep accepted; if later worse, current c may become >best_c but best saved.
      stagn=0
   else:
      apply_flip(a,c,n,q,-1.0)
      apply_flip(a,c,n,p,1.0)
      stagn += 1
      if stagn > 1000:
         # resync to best_a to avoid drift
         a=best_a.copy(); c=corr(a,n); curM=best_c; stagn=0
 return best_a, best_c*(2.0/n)
```
Potential issue: If accept equal random walks, current c may increase later because we only check newM< or == at acceptance; but each accepted move had newM == curM (not >). However future moves may increase; then reject. So current M never exceeds last accepted curM? If accepted equal, curM remains; next move can make M larger, reject. So safe. But due to recompute, best_c maybe. If stagn resets to best, okay.
But active filtering uses curM; if c contains entries >curM due to numeric, active includes all >=. Good.

```
def round_balanced(h,n):
    target=n//2
    order=np.argsort(h)
    a=np.zeros(n)
    if target>0: a[order[n-target:]]=1.0
    return a
```

Now smooth L-BFGS helpers:
```
def smooth_obj(x,n,alpha,rho):
  dx=2.0/n
  c=np.correlate(x,1-x,'full')
  m=float(np.max(c))
  s=(c-m)/alpha
  lse=m + alpha*(float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(s))))) # s max 0, ok
  return dx*lse + rho*(float(x.sum())-n/2.0)**2
```
If alpha small, exp okay since s≤0. Could overflow? no. `np.sum(exp(s))` length 2n-1 ~2000, fine.

```
def smooth_lbfgs(h,n,deadline):
 h=project(np.array(h,dtype=float,copy=True),n)
 best_h=h.copy(); best_c=c5_of(h,n)
 alphas=(3000.0,700.0,150.0)
 for al in alphas:
    if time.time()>deadline-10: break
    bounds=[(0.0,1.0)]*n
    try:
       res=minimize(smooth_obj, best_h, args=(n,al,10000.0), method='L-BFGS-B', bounds=bounds, options={'maxiter':80,'ftol':1e-13,'gtol':1e-9,'maxls':20})
    except Exception:
       continue
    new=project(res.x,n)
    c=c5_of(new,n)
    if c < best_c:
       best_h, best_c = new, c
       # small CD
       if time.time() < deadline-10:
          new, c = cd_fast(new,n,5,time.time()+min(30,deadline-time.time()),recomp=1)
          if c<best_c: best_h,best_c=new,c
 return best_h,best_c
```

Start generation:
```
def make_start(parent_h, parent_n, n, rng, mode):
   if mode==0 and parent_h is not None:
      if parent_n==n: h=parent_h.copy()
      else: h=np.interp(np.linspace(0,2,n), np.linspace(0,2,parent_n), parent_h)
   elif mode==1: h=0.5+0.35*rng.normal(0,1,n)
   elif mode==2:
      # Kronecker golden
      alpha=(np.sqrt(2.0)-1.0)
      h=(np.mod(np.arange(n)*alpha,1.0)<0.5).astype(float)
   elif mode==3:
      # thue
      h=thue_bits(n).astype(float)
   elif mode==4:
      # sinusoid
      fr=round(0.5 + 5*rng.rand())? But deterministic formula. Use f=4+ (n%5)
      x=np.linspace(0,2,n)
      h=0.5+0.5*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*x + rng.rand()*6)
   else:
      # random block half
      h=rng.rand(n)
   return project(h,n)
```
Need no long lists. Thue bits helper.
Maybe mode block hierarchical: `h = (np.arange(n)//block) % 2` with block ~ max(1,n//8). Good.

### project
Need robust but not too slow. Use proportion.
```
def project(h,n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    if h.size != n:
       # if wrong size, interp? assume
    np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    target = 0.5*n
    for _ in range(80):
       diff = target - h.sum()
       if abs(diff) < 1e-13: break
       if diff > 0:
          mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-15
          if not mask.any(): break
          room = 1.0 - h[mask]
          tot = room.sum()
          if tot <= 1e-15: break
          h[mask] += (diff/tot)*room
       else:
          mask = h > 1e-15
          if not mask.any(): break
          tot = h[mask].sum()
          if tot <= 1e-15: break
          h[mask] += (diff/tot)*h[mask]
       np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
       mask = (h > 1e-9) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-9)
       if mask.any():
          m = int(np.count_nonzero(mask))
          h[mask] += diff/m
          np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
       else:
          h += diff/n
          np.clip(h,0.0,1.0,out=h)
    return h
```
If final diff after clip still small. Good.

### cd_check
Need use float arrays; if b_pos size zero. Tolerance.
```
def cd_check(a_pos,b_pos,a_neg,b_neg,z_start,diag_base,diag_h0,lo,hi,t):
    if t < z_start - 1e-11: return False, lo, hi, 0.0, 0.0
    low=lo; high=hi
    if b_pos.size:
       ub = float(np.min((t - b_pos)/a_pos))
       if ub < high: high = ub
    if b_neg.size:
       lb = float(np.max((t - b_neg)/a_neg))
       if lb > low: low = lb
    if low > high + 1e-11: return False, lo, hi, 0.0, 0.0
    qb = 1.0 - 2.0*diag_h0
    qmax = diag_base + 0.25 - diag_h0*diag_h0
    if qmax <= t + 1e-11: return True, low, high, 0.0,0.0
    disc = qb*qb + 4.0*(diag_base - t)
    if disc < 0.0: disc=0.0
    rt = float(np.sqrt(disc))
    r1 = 0.5*(qb - rt); r2 = 0.5*(qb + rt)
    if high < r1 or low > r2: return True, low, high, r1, r2
    if low < r1 - 1e-12 or high > r2 + 1e-12: return True, low, high, r1, r2
    return False, lo, hi, r1, r2
```
If diag restriction not active (r1,r2 zeros), candidate list duplicates.

### cd_eval
Simple.

### cd_fast
Add max_step.
```
def cd_fast(h,n,max_passes,deadline,max_step=0.2,recomp=1):
    h = project(np.array(h,dtype=float,copy=True),n)
    c = corr(h,n)
    dx=2.0/n
    best_c = float(np.max(c))*dx
    for _ in range(max_passes):
       if time.time() > deadline: break
       cmax = float(np.max(c))
       if cmax <= 1e-15: break
       active = c >= (cmax - 1e-10)
       improved=False
       for p in range(n):
          if time.time()>deadline: break
          S = p if p >= n-1-p else n-1-p
          m0=n-1-S; m1=n-1+S
          if not np.any(active[m0:m1+1]): continue
          h0=float(h[p])
          if h0 <= 1e-13 or h0 >= 1.0-1e-13: continue
          ms=np.arange(m0,m1+1)
          s=ms-(n-1)
          aff=c[ms].copy()
          coeff=np.zeros(len(ms))
          vh=p-s; mask=(vh>=0)&(vh<n)
          if np.any(mask): coeff[mask] += 1.0 - h[vh[mask]]
          vl=p+s; mask=(vl>=0)&(vl<n)
          if np.any(mask): coeff[mask] -= h[vl[mask]]
          di=S; coeff[di]=0.0
          diag_base=float(c[n-1])
          unaff=0.0
          if m0>0: unaff=max(unaff,float(c[:m0].max()))
          if m1+1 < 2*n-1: unaff=max(unaff,float(c[m1+1:].max()))
          aff_max=float(aff.max())
          if aff_max <= unaff + 1e-14: continue
          f0=max(unaff,aff_max)
          lo=-h0; hi=1.0-h0
          if max_step>0:
             lo=max(lo,-max_step); hi=min(hi,max_step)
             if lo>=hi: continue
          posm=coeff>1e-14; negm=coeff<-1e-14
          a_pos=coeff[posm]; b_pos=aff[posm]
          a_neg=coeff[negm]; b_neg=aff[negm]
          zerom=~(posm|negm); zerom[di]=False
          b_zero_max = float(aff[zerom].max()) if np.any(zerom) else -1e100
          z_start=max(unaff,b_zero_max,0.0)
          t_hi=f0
          t_lo=z_start
          if t_lo > t_hi: t_lo=t_hi
          if t_hi - t_lo > 1e-13:
             for __ in range(22):
                tmid=0.5*(t_lo+t_hi)
                feasible,_,_,_,_ = cd_check(a_pos,b_pos,a_neg,b_neg,z_start,diag_base,h0,lo,hi,tmid)
                if feasible: t_hi=tmid
                else: t_lo=tmid
          else:
             t_hi=t_lo
          feas,low,high,r1,r2 = cd_check(a_pos,b_pos,a_neg,b_neg,z_start,diag_base,h0,lo,hi,t_hi)
          if not feas:
             feas,low,high,r1,r2 = cd_check(a_pos,b_pos,a_neg,b_pos? wait b_pos..., f0)
```

```
          best_d=0.0; best_f=f0
```

Now, design `make_start`:
```
def make_start(parent,pn,n,rng,mode):
    if parent is not None and pn == n and mode == 0:
        return project(parent,n)
    if parent is not None and mode == 0:
        x0=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,pn)
        x1=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
        h=np.interp(x1,x0,parent.astype(float))
        return project(h,n)
    if mode == 1:
        h=0.5 + 0.45*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
    elif mode == 2:
        alpha=np.sqrt(2.0)-1.0
        h=(np.mod(np.arange(n)*alpha,1.0) < 0.5).astype(float)
        h = h + 0.08*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
    elif mode == 3:
        h=thue_bits(n).astype(float)
        h = h + 0.08*rng.normal(0.0,1.0,n)
    else:
        x=np.linspace(0.0,2.0,n)
        h=0.5 + 0.5*np.sin(np.pi*(3.0 + float(n % 5))*x)
    return project(h,n)
```
Mode 0 with parent None falls through? If parent is None and mode0, maybe use random? In code if parent not None and mode0; else if mode0 and parent None, set h=0.5+... We'll handle:
```
if mode==0 and parent is not None: ... return
if mode==0: h=0.5+0.4*rng.normal...
```

`thue_bits` top-level:
```
def thue_bits(n):
    if n <= 0: return np.array([], dtype=float)
    t=np.array([0], dtype=int)
    while t.size < n:
       t=np.concatenate((t, 1-t))
    return t[:n].astype(float)
```

```
def run(seed=42,budget_s=1100,**kwargs):
    t0=time.time(); deadline=t0+float(budget_s)-10.0
    rng=np.random.RandomState(seed)
    parent=None; pn=0
    try:
       parent=np.asarray(initial_h_values,dtype=float)
       if parent.size>0: pn=int(parent.size)
       else: parent=None
    except Exception:
       parent=None; pn=0
    n_list=[]
    if pn>0 and 50<=pn<=1500:
       n_list.append(pn)
    for v in (350,500,700,1000):
       if v not in n_list:
          n_list.append(v)
    # maybe cap >1200? if parent 1500, include; dx smaller. Correlate n=1500 ~2.25M, okay but CD active skip. Could include but time. Cap to 1200? If parent 700 fine.
    n_list=[v for v in n_list if 80 <= v <= 1200]
    if not n_list: n_list=[700]
    # sort: parent first, then increasing? maybe keep.
    # weights
    weights=[]
    for i,n in enumerate(n_list):
       if i==0: w=1.8
       elif n <= 700: w=1.1
       else: w=0.8
       weights.append(float(w))
    total_w=sum(weights)
    best_h=None; best_c=1e9
    for idx,n in enumerate(n_list):
       rem=deadline-time.time()
       if rem < 70: break
       share=rem*weights[idx]/total_w
       if share < 35.0: share=35.0
       if share > rem: share=rem
       dl=time.time()+share
       h=make_start(parent,pn,n,rng,0)
       h,c=cd_fast(h,n,1000,dl,max_step=0.35)
       local_h,local_c=h,c
       if c<best_c: best_h, best_c = h.copy(), c
       # refine same
       if time.time() < dl - 15:
          h2,c2=cd_fast(h,n,1000,dl,max_step=0.07)
          if c2<local_c: local_h,local_c=h2,c2
          if c2<best_c: best_h,best_c=h2.copy(),c2
       # alternative start if time
       if time.time() < dl - 30:
          mode = 2 if (n%2==1) else 3
          # maybe for even use 2 Kronecker? but binary search later. Use mode2 for Kronecker, mode1 random.
          mode = 1 if idx % 2 == 0 else 2
          h3=make_start(parent,pn,n,rng,mode)
          h3,c3=cd_fast(h3,n,600,dl,max_step=0.25)
          if c3<local_c: local_h,local_c=h3,c3
          if c3<best_c: best_h,best_c=h3.copy(),c3
          # refine
          if time.time() < dl - 20:
             h4,c4=cd_fast(h3,n,600,dl,max_step=0.05)
             if c4<local_c: local_h,local_c=h4,c4
             if c4<best_c: best_h,best_c=h4.copy(),c4
    # binary polish on best if even and time
    if best_h is not None:
       nbest=len(best_h)
       if nbest % 2 == 0 and time.time() < deadline - 70:
          dl_bin=time.time()+min(220.0, max(30.0, (deadline-time.time())*0.25))
          a=round_balanced(best_h,nbest)
          a,c= binary_targeted(a,nbest,dl_bin,seed=123)
          if c < best_c:
             best_h=a; best_c=c
       # final CD two stages if time
       if time.time() < deadline - 30:
          h4,c4=cd_fast(best_h,nbest,1000,deadline,max_step=0.2)
          if c4<best_c: best_h,best_c=h4.copy(),c4
          if time.time() < deadline - 15:
             h5,c5=cd_fast(best_h,nbest,1000,deadline,max_step=0.04)
             if c5<best_c: best_h,best_c=h5.copy(),c5
    if best_h is None:
       # fallback
       h=np.full(700,0.5); h=project(h,700); best_c=c5_of(h,700); best_h=h
    best_h=project(best_h,len(best_h))
    best_c=c5_of(best_h,len(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c, len(best_h)
```


```python
import numpy as np
import time


def project(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float).copy()
    np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    target = 0.5 * n
    for _ in range(80):
        diff = target - h.sum()
        if abs(diff) < 1e-13:
            break
        if diff > 0.0:
            mask = h < 1.0 - 1e-15
            if not mask.any():
                break
            room = 1.0 - h[mask]
            tot = room.sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h[mask] += (diff / tot) * room
        else:
            mask = h > 1e-15
            if not mask.any():
                break
            tot = h[mask].sum()
            if tot <= 1e-15:
                break
            h[mask] += (diff / tot) * h[mask]
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    diff = target - h.sum()
    if abs(diff) > 1e-14:
        mask = (h > 1e-9) & (h < 1.0 - 1e-9)
        if mask.any():
            h[mask] += diff / float(np.count_nonzero(mask))
        else:
            h += diff / float(n)
        np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0, out=h)
    return h


def corr_full(h):
    return np.correlate(h, 1.0 - h, mode="full")


def c5_of(h, n):
    return float(np.max(corr_full(h)) * (2.0 / n))


def cd_eval(aff, coeff, diag_base, diag_h0, di, unaff, d):
    vals = aff + coeff * d
    vals[di] = diag_base + d * (1.0 - 2.0 * diag_h0 - d)
    r = float(vals.max())
    if unaff > r:
        r = unaff
    return r


def cd_check(a_pos, b_pos, a_neg, b_neg, z_start, diag_base, diag_h0, lo, hi, t):
    if t < z_start - 1e-11:
        return False, lo, hi, 0.0, 0.0
    low, high = lo, hi
    if b_pos.size:
        ub = float(np.min((t - b_pos) / a_pos))
        if ub < high:
            high = ub
    if b_neg.size:
        lb = float(np.max((t - b_neg) / a_neg))
        if lb > low:
            low = lb
    if low > high + 1e-11:
        return False, lo, hi, 0.0, 0.0
    qb = 1.0 - 2.0 * diag_h0
    qmax = diag_base + 0.25 - diag_h0 * diag_h0
    if qmax <= t + 1e-11:
        return True, low, high, 0.0, 0.0
    disc = qb * qb + 4.0 * (diag_base - t)
    if disc < 0.0:
        disc = 0.0
    rt = float(np.sqrt(disc))
    r1 = 0.5 * (qb - rt)
    r2 = 0.5 * (qb + rt)
    if high < r1 or low > r2:
        return True, low, high, r1, r2
    if low < r1 - 1e-12 or high > r2 + 1e-12:
        return True, low, high, r1, r2
    return False, lo, hi, r1, r2


def cd_fast(h, n, max_passes, deadline, max_step=0.15):
    h = project(np.array(h, dtype=float, copy=True), n)
    c = corr_full(h)
    dx = 2.0 / n
    best_c = float(np.max(c)) * dx

    for _ in range(max_passes):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        cmax = float(np.max(c))
        if cmax <= 1e-15:
            break
        active = c >= (cmax - 1e-10)
        improved = False

        for p in range(n):
            if time.time() > deadline:
                break
            S = p if p >= n - 1 - p else n - 1 - p
            m0 = n - 1 - S
            m1 = n - 1 + S
            if not np.any(active[m0:m1 + 1]):
                continue

            h0 = float(h[p])
            ms = np.arange(m0, m1 + 1)
            s = ms - (n - 1)
            aff = c[ms].copy()
            coeff = np.zeros(len(ms), dtype=float)

            vh = p - s
            mask = (vh >= 0) & (vh < n)
            if mask.any():
                coeff[mask] += 1.0 - h[vh[mask]]
            vl = p + s
            mask = (vl >= 0) & (vl < n)
            if mask.any():
                coeff[mask] -= h[vl[mask]]

            di = S
            coeff[di] = 0.0
            diag_base = float(c[n - 1])

            unaff = 0.0
            if m0 > 0:
                unaff = max(unaff, float(c[:m0].max()))
            if m1 + 1 < 2 * n - 1:
                unaff = max(unaff, float(c[m1 + 1:].max()))

            aff_max = float(aff.max())
            if aff_max <= unaff + 1e-14:
                continue

            f0 = max(unaff, aff_max)
            lo = -h0
            hi = 1.0 - h0
            if max_step > 0.0:
                lo = max(lo, -max_step)
                hi = min(hi, max_step)
            if lo >= hi - 1e-15:
                continue

            posm = coeff > 1e-14
            negm = coeff < -1e-14
            a_pos = coeff[posm]
            b_pos = aff[posm]
            a_neg = coeff[negm]
            b_neg = aff[negm]

            zerom = ~(posm | negm)
            zerom[di] = False
            b_zero_max = float(aff[zerom].max()) if zero_mask_any(zerom) else -1e100
            z_start = max(unaff, b_zero_max, 0.0)

            t_lo, t_hi = z_start, f0
            if t_lo > t_hi:
                t_lo = t_hi
            if t_hi - t_lo > 1e-13:
                for __ in range(22):
                    tm = 0.5 * (t_lo + t_hi)
                    ok, _, _, _, _ = cd_check(a_pos, b_pos, a_neg, b_neg, z_start, diag_base, h0, lo, hi, tm)
                    if ok:
                        t_hi = tm
                    else:
                        t_lo = tm
            else:
                t_hi = t_lo

            feas, low, high, r1, r2 = cd_check(a_pos, b_pos, a_neg, b_neg, z_start, diag_base, h0, lo, hi, t_hi)
            if not feas:
                feas, low, high, r1, r2 = cd_check(a_pos, b_pos, a_neg, b_neg, z_start, diag_base, h0, lo, hi, f0)

            cands = [lo, hi, 0.0, 0.5 * (lo + hi), 0.5 - h0,
                     low, high, 0.5 * (low + high),
                     max(lo, min(high, r1)), min(hi, max(low, r2)),
                     min(hi, r1 - 1e-9), max(lo, r2 + 1e-9)]
            best_d = 0.0
            best_f = f0
            for d in cands:
                if d < lo:
                    d = lo
                elif d > hi:
                    d = hi
                f = cd_eval(aff, coeff, diag_base, h0, di, unaff, d)
                if f < best_f:
                    best_f = f
                    best_d = d

            if best_f < f0 - 1e-11 and abs(best_d) > 1e-13:
                h[p] = h0 + best_d
                c[ms] = aff + coeff * best_d
                c[n - 1] = diag_base + best_d * (1.0 - 2.0 * h0 - best_d)
                if c[n - 1] < 0.0 and c[n - 1] > -1e-10:
                    c[n - 1] = 0.0
                improved = True

        h = project(h, n)
        c = corr_full(h)
        cur_c = float(np.max(c)) * dx
        if cur_c < best_c:
            best_c = cur_c
        if not improved:
            break

    h = project(h, n)
    c = corr_full(h)
    return h, float(np.max(c)) * dx


def zero_mask_any(mask):
    return bool(np.any(mask))


def thue_bits(n):
    if n <= 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=float)
    t = np.array([0], dtype=int)
    while t.size < n:
        t = np.concatenate((t, 1 - t))
    return t[:n].astype(float)


def make_start(parent, pn, n, rng, mode):
    if mode == 0 and parent is not None and pn == n:
        return project(parent, n)
    if mode == 0 and parent is not None and pn > 0:
        x0 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, pn)
        x1 = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
        return project(np.interp(x1, x0, parent.astype(float)), n)

    if mode == 1 or (parent is None and mode == 0):
        h = 0.5 + 0.45 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    elif mode == 2:
        alpha = np.sqrt(2.0) - 1.0
        h = (np.mod(np.arange(n) * alpha, 1.0) < 0.5).astype(float)
        h += 0.07 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    elif mode == 3:
        h = thue_bits(n)
        h += 0.07 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    else:
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 2.0, n)
        h = 0.5 + 0.5 * np.sin(np.pi * (3.0 + float(n % 5)) * x)
        h += 0.03 * rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, n)
    return project(h, n)


def round_balanced(h, n):
    h = np.asarray(h, dtype=float)
    target = n // 2
    order = np.argsort(h)
    a = np.zeros(n, dtype=float)
    if target > 0:
        a[order[n - target:]] = 1.0
    return a


def apply_flip(a, c, n, p, dh):
    val = int(a[p] > 0.5)
    if val == 1 and dh >= 0:
        return
    if val == 0 and dh <= 0:
        return
    S = p if p >= n - 1 - p else n - 1 - p
    ms = np.arange(n - 1 - S, n - 1 + S + 1)
    s = ms - (n - 1)
    coeff = np.zeros(len(ms), dtype=float)

    vh = p - s
    mask = (vh >= 0) & (vh < n)
    if mask.any():
        coeff[mask] += 1.0 - a[vh[mask]]
    vl = p + s
    mask = (vl >= 0) & (vl < n)
    if mask.any():
        coeff[mask] -= a[vl[mask]]

    coeff[S] = 0.0
    c[ms] += coeff * dh
    c[n - 1] = 0.0
    a[p] = 1.0 - val


def binary_targeted(a, n, deadline, seed=123):
    a = np.array(a, dtype=float, copy=True)
    target = n // 2
    if int(round(a.sum())) != target:
        a = round_balanced(a, n)
    c = corr_full(a)
    curM = int(round(float(np.max(c))))
    best_c = curM
    best_a = a.copy()
    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)
    stagn = 0

    for it in range(30000):
        if time.time() > deadline:
            break
        if it % 120 == 119:
            c = corr_full(a)

        curM = int(round(float(np.max(c))))
        if curM < best_c:
            best_c = curM
            best_a = a.copy()
        if curM <= 0:
            break

        active = np.where(c >= curM)[0]
        if active.size == 0:
            break
        m = int(active[int(rng.randint(active.size))])
        s = m - (n - 1)
        lo = max(0, s)
        hi = min(n, n + s)
        if hi <= lo:
            continue
        ps = np.arange(lo, hi)
        ps = ps[(a[ps] > 0.5) & (a[ps - s] <= 0.5)]
        if ps.size == 0:
            stagn += 5
            if stagn > 120:
                c = corr_full(a)
                curM = int(round(float(np.max(c))))
                if curM < best_c:
                    best_c = curM
                    best_a = a.copy()
                break
            continue

        p = int(ps[int(rng.randint(ps.size))])
        q = p - s
        if q < 0 or q >= n or a[q] > 0.5 or p == q:
            continue

        apply_flip(a, c, n, p, -1.0)
        apply_flip(a, c, n, q, 1.0)
        newM = int(round(float(np.max(c))))

        if newM < curM:
            if newM < best_c:
                best_c = newM
                best_a = a.copy()
            stagn = 0
        elif newM == curM and stagn > 250 and rng.rand() < 0.12:
            stagn = 0
        else:
            apply_flip(a, c, n, q, -1.0)
            apply_flip(a, c, n, p, 1.0)
            stagn += 1
            if stagn > 1200:
                a = best_a.copy()
                c = corr_full(a)
                curM = best_c
                stagn = 0

    return best_a, float(best_c) * (2.0 / n)


def run(seed=42, budget_s=1100, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.time()
    total_budget = float(budget_s)
    end = t0 + total_budget - 5.0
    reserve = min(260.0, max(80.0, total_budget * 0.22))
    loop_end = t0 + total_budget - 5.0 - reserve

    rng = np.random.RandomState(seed)

    parent = None
    pn = 0
    try:
        parent = np.asarray(initial_h_values, dtype=float)
        if parent.size > 0:
            pn = int(parent.size)
        else:
            parent = None
    except Exception:
        parent = None
        pn = 0

    n_list = []
    if pn > 0 and 80 <= pn <= 1400:
        n_list.append(pn)
    for v in (350, 500, 700, 1000):
        if v not in n_list:
            n_list.append(v)
    n_list = [v for v in n_list if 80 <= v <= 1400]
    if not n_list:
        n_list = [700]

    weights = []
    for i, n in enumerate(n_list):
        if i == 0:
            w = 1.8
        elif n <= 700:
            w = 1.1
        else:
            w = 0.8
        weights.append(float(w))
    total_w = float(sum(weights))

    best_h = None
    best_c = 1e100

    for idx, n in enumerate(n_list):
        rem = loop_end - time.time()
        if rem < 60.0:
            break
        share = rem * weights[idx] / total_w
        share = max(30.0, share)
        if share > rem:
            share = rem
        dl = time.time() + share

        h = make_start(parent, pn, n, rng, 0)
        h, c = cd_fast(h, n, 1000, dl, max_step=0.16)
        local_h, local_c = h, c
        if c < best_c:
            best_h, best_c = h.copy(), c

        if time.time() < dl - 15.0:
            h2, c2 = cd_fast(h, n, 1000, dl, max_step=0.055)
            if c2 < local_c:
                local_h, local_c = h2, c2
            if c2 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h2.copy(), c2

        if time.time() < dl - 35.0:
            mode = 1 if idx % 2 == 0 else 2
            h3 = make_start(parent, pn, n, rng, mode)
            h3, c3 = cd_fast(h3, n, 700, dl, max_step=0.14)
            if c3 < local_c:
                local_h, local_c = h3, c3
            if c3 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h3.copy(), c3
            if time.time() < dl - 20.0:
                h4, c4 = cd_fast(h3, n, 700, dl, max_step=0.045)
                if c4 < local_c:
                    local_h, local_c = h4, c4
                if c4 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = h4.copy(), c4

    if best_h is not None:
        nbest = len(best_h)

        if nbest % 2 == 0 and time.time() < end - 45.0:
            dl_bin = time.time() + min(220.0, max(25.0, (end - time.time()) * 0.45))
            a = round_balanced(best_h, nbest)
            a, cb = binary_targeted(a, nbest, dl_bin, seed=171)
            if cb < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = a.copy(), cb

        if time.time() < end - 25.0:
            h5, c5 = cd_fast(best_h, nbest, 1000, end, max_step=0.12)
            if c5 < best_c:
                best_h, best_c = h5.copy(), c5
            if time.time() < end - 12.0:
                h6, c6 = cd_fast(best_h, nbest, 1000, end, max_step=0.035)
                if c6 < best_c:
                    best_h, best_c = h6.copy(), c6

    if best_h is None:
        best_h = np.full(700, 0.5)
        best_h = project(best_h, 700)
        best_c = c5_of(best_h, 700)

    best_h = project(best_h, len(best_h))
    best_c = c5_of(best_h, len(best_h))
    return best_h, best_c, len(best_h)
```
